i DIRECTORY

LIVE TVVIDEO STORE

HOW IT WORKS

LIVE TV

Channels run on a shared schedule. Tune in and join the program already in progress. The guide shows what’s playing and what comes next.

VIDEO STORE

Pick a movie and start it from the beginning at Aardvark Video.

THE RECORDINGS

All programs play from Internet Archive except the BCVHS channel, which is played from YouTube. Some tapes include their original commercials.

THE NUMBERS

Counts represent recordings in rotation. A recording can be an episode, movie, short clip or a tape containing several programs. Some recordings appear on more than one channel.

IF SOMETHING WON’T PLAY

Archive files can load slowly or become temporarily unavailable. Try another channel and come back.

THE LINEUP
